```json
{
  "path": "ios/Sources/GameWebView.swift",
  "instructions": "Main WKWebView wrapper: loads live game site, falls back to bundled offline assets, custom UA hides the download-APK bar",
  "content": "import SwiftUI\nimport WebKit\n\n/// The whole app: a WKWebView pointed at the game.\n///\n/// Mirrors the Android wrapper's behaviour:\n///  - load the live game (https://mantarjantar.com) for online multiplayer;\n///  - if that can't be reached (offline), fall back to the bundled copy of the\n///    web client, where Practice (offline) mode works with no network at all;\n///  - append \"JantarMantarChaloApp\" to the user agent so the page hides its\n///    \"Get the Android app\" banner (it checks the UA for that string).\nstruct GameWebView: UIViewRepresentable {\n    static let liveURL = URL(string: \"https://mantarjantar.com\")!\n\n    func makeCoordinator() -> Coordinator { Coordinator() }\n\n    func makeUIView(context: Context) -> WKWebView {\n        let config = WKWebViewConfiguration()\n        config.websiteDataStore = .default()\n        config.applicationNameForUserAgent = \"JantarMantarChaloApp\"\n        // Web Audio (the game's sound) should never require an extra media element gesture.\n        config.mediaTypesRequiringUserActionForPlayback = []\n        if let assetsRoot = Bundle.main.url(forResource: \"WebAssets\", withExtension: nil) {\n            config.setURLSchemeHandler(\n                LocalAssetSchemeHandler(assetsRoot: assetsRoot),\n                forURLScheme: LocalAssetSchemeHandler.scheme\n            )\n        }\n\n        let webView = WKWebView(frame: .zero, configuration: config)\n        webView.navigationDelegate = context.coordinator\n        webView.scrollView.isScrollEnabled = false\n        webView.scrollView.bounces = false\n        webView.scrollView.contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior = .never\n        webView.scrollView.showsHorizontalScrollIndicator = false\n        webView.scrollView.showsVerticalScrollIndicator = false\n        webView.allowsBackForwardNavigationGestures = false\n        webView.isOpaque = false\n        // Matches the page's own background so there's no white flash while loading.\n        webView.backgroundColor = UIColor(red: 0.561, green: 0.682, blue: 0.388, alpha: 1)\n\n        // It's a game: keep the screen awake, like the Android app's keep-screen-on flag.\n        UIApplication.shared.isIdleTimerDisabled = true\n\n        context.coordinator.webView = webView\n        context.coordinator.loadInitial()\n        return webView\n    }\n\n    func updateUIView(_ uiView: WKWebView, context: Context) {}\n\n    static func dismantleUIView(_ uiView: WKWebView, coordinator: Coordinator) {\n        NotificationCenter.default.removeObserver(coordinator)\n    }\n\n    final class Coordinator: NSObject, WKNavigationDelegate {\n        weak var webView: WKWebView?\n        private var usingLocalFallback = false\n\n        override init() {\n            super.init()\n            NotificationCenter.default.addObserver(\n                self, selector: #selector(reloadGame), name: .jmcReload, object: nil\n            )\n        }\n\n        @objc private func reloadGame() {\n            usingLocalFallback = false\n            loadInitial()\n        }\n\n        func loadInitial() {\n            if ProcessInfo.processInfo.arguments.contains(\"-JMCOffline\") {\n                // Debug hook: force the bundled copy (used to test the offline path).\n                usingLocalFallback = true\n                webView?.load(LocalAssetSchemeHandler.localIndexRequest())\n            } else {\n                webView?.load(URLRequest(url: GameWebView.liveURL))\n            }\n        }\n\n        // The live site can't be reached (no network, DNS failure, timeout):\n        // switch to the bundled copy so Practice offline still works.\n        func webView(\n            _ webView: WKWebView, didFailProvisionalNavigation navigation: WKNavigation!,\n            withError error: Error\n        ) {\n            let ns = error as NSError\n            guard ns.domain == NSURLErrorDomain,\n                  ns.code != NSURLErrorCancelled,\n                  !usingLocalFallback,\n                  let assetsRoot = Bundle.main.url(forResource: \"WebAssets\", withExtension: nil)\n            else { return }\n            let failedURL = (ns.userInfo[NSURLErrorFailingURLErrorKey] as? URL) ?? webView.url\n            guard failedURL?.host?.hasSuffix(\"mantarjantar.com\") == true else { return }\n            _ = assetsRoot\n            usingLocalFallback = true\n            webView.load(LocalAssetSchemeHandler.localIndexRequest())\n        }\n\n        // Links with target=\"_blank\" (e.g. \"About the game\") open outside the app.\n        func webView(\n            _ webView: WKWebView, createWebViewWith configuration: WKWebViewConfiguration,\n            for navigationAction: WKNavigationAction,\n            windowFeatures: WKWindowFeatures\n        ) -> WKWebView? {\n            if navigationAction.targetFrame == nil, let url = navigationAction.request.url {\n                UIApplication.shared.open(url)\n            }\n            return nil\n        }\n\n        func webView(\n            _ webView: WKWebView, decidePolicyFor navigationAction: WKNavigationAction,\n            decisionHandler: @escaping (WKNavigationActionPolicy) -> Void\n        ) {\n            guard let url = navigationAction.request.url else {\n                decisionHandler(.cancel)\n                return\n            }\n            let scheme = url.scheme?.lowercased() ?? \"\"\n            if [\"http\", \"https\", LocalAssetSchemeHandler.scheme, \"about\", \"blob\", \"data\"].contains(scheme) {\n                decisionHandler(.allow)\n            } else {\n                UIApplication.shared.open(url)\n                decisionHandler(.cancel)\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

> 
> 2. Very interesting: plain HTTP to mantarjantar.com redirects to `https://www.safebrowse.io/warn.html?url=...` — some network-level proxy (safebrowse) on this machine/network is intercepting and blocking. And TLS handshake fails. So this network actively blocks the game site (maybe school/work network content filter). So in the simulator the live load failed → didFailProvisionalNavigation should have fired → fallback to local assets. But the screen is solid green = my background color, meaning nothing rendered, not even the fallback.
> 
> Wait — maybe the screenshot was taken too early, or the fallback failed. Let me think: if live load failed with TLS error (NSURLErrorSecureConnectionFailed, code -1206) — domain NSURLErrorDomain, code != cancelled — should pass my guard. failedURL host mantarjantar.com — hasSuffix "mantarjantar.com" true. Then load local index. Local index → jmclocal://app/public/index.html → scheme handler serves. That should render the menu (with "Opened as a file" status). Solid green suggests the local load didn't render either... OR the screenshot was taken while things were still in flight (8s should be enough).
> 
> Hmm — or the initial load never failed because the error came as a different delegate callback: for TLS/cert errors, WKWebView stops and calls didFailProvisionalNavigation. Should be fine.

> Another possibility: the page DID load (either copy) but rendering requires more time? 8s is plenty.

> _Reasoning_
> The offline (-JMCOffline) launch renders the full menu: paper #f6eedd (246,238,221), role card colors #fffaf0, selected role #fff3e0, button saffron (255,153,51), practice button brown (109,90,68). So the scheme handler + bundled assets work perfectly.
> 
> So the earlier solid-green screenshot in normal mode = live site load failed (network blocks mantarjantar.com on this network — TLS intercepted, redirected to safebrowse warning) AND the fallback didn't trigger. Why not?
> 
> Possible reasons:
> 1. The TLS failure surfaces via `didFailProvisionalNavigation` — should work... unless the error is delivered via `webViewWebContentProcessDidTerminate` or as a different navigation error (didFail). Or the error domain isn't NSURLErrorDomain (it could be, e.g., NSURLErrorDomain code -1206 secure connection failed — that's fine).
> 
> 2. OR the page actually loaded a *redirect* to safebrowse warn page (http:// mantarjantar.com → 302 to safebrowse)? We load https://. With TLS handshake failure, no redirect happens.
> 
> 3. Wait — maybe the load didn't fail at all: the machine's network intercepts; maybe WebKit got the TLS error quickly and my guard `failedURL?.host?.hasSuffix("mantarjantar.com")` — `webView.url` during provisional failure might be the failing URL... but `(ns.userInfo[NSURLErrorFailingURLErrorKey] as? URL) ?? webView.url` should give mantarjantar.com. Unless userInfo key is NSURLErrorFailingURLStringErrorKey (string) not the URL key. I use `as? URL` on NSURLErrorFailingURLErrorKey; if absent, fall back to webView.url, which during a provisional failure... WKWebView.url may still be nil since the previous load was about:blank? Then guard fails → no fallback. That's plausibly the bug!
> 
> Actually NSURLErrorFailingURLErrorKey is normally present for NSURLErrorDomain errors. But WKWebView wraps errors; the failing URL might be under `NSErrorFailingURLKey`? For WebKit, the userInfo usually contains NSURLErrorFailingURLErrorKey too. Hmm.
> 
> 4. Another possibility: the initial navigation's failure happens *before* didFailProvisionalNavigation is called on my delegate because... makeUIView: I call `context.coordinator.loadInitial()` in makeUIView — webView.navigationDelegate was set before, ok.
> 
> 5. Or the app crashed on the failure path? The first screenshot showed green — no crash (screen would be home screen). Actually the app was still running showing green webview.
> 
> Let me debug properly: check the simulator system log for that session — WebKit network process logs would show what happened. Better: temporarily instrument the Coordinator to log to os.Logger / print, and run with --console to see output. `simctl launch --console` streams stdout/stderr. print() from the app goes there.
> 
> Plan: add a few debug prints in GameWebView coordinator (didFailProvisionalNavigation: print error domain/code/userInfo and whether fallback triggered). Also in scheme handler: print served paths. Rebuild, launch --console with normal args, inspect output. Then fix accordingly and also verify offline fallback triggers when network is blocked.
> 
> Actually, let me think about what error occurs with this safebrowse proxy: TLS alert protocol version — WebKit might report NSURLErrorSecureConnectionFailed (-1206) or NSURLErrorClientCertificateRejected (-1205), or maybe an "App Transport Security" style error domain NSURLErrorDomain... fine.
> 
> Hmm wait — one more candidate cause: `didFailProvisionalNavigation` may not fire because the failure is a *redirect* to safebrowse over http→https? The proxy could be doing TLS MITM with an untrusted cert → WebKit reports error but for HTTPS handshake failure it's still provisional nav failure.
> 
> Let me just add logging and test. Quick iteration:
> - print in loadInitial which URL.
> - print in didFailProvisionalNavigation: error localizedDescription, domain, code, failing URL keys, webView.url.
> - print in decidePolicyFor (url) — see navigation sequence.
> - print in scheme handler serve/fail.
> 
> Then rebuild & run --console, capture output.

{
  "path": "ios/Sources/JMCLog.swift",
  "instructions": "File-based debug logger for simulator verification",
  "content": "import Foundation\n\n/// Tiny file logger for debugging in the simulator (visible via\n/// `simctl get_app_container <device> <bundle-id> data` + tmp/jmc.log).\nfunc jmcLog(_ message: String) {\n    let url = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent(\"jmc.log\")\n    let data = (message + \"\\n\").data(using: .utf8) ?? Data()\n    if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path),\n       let fh = try? FileHandle(forWritingTo: url) {\n        defer { try? fh.close() }\n        _ = try? fh.seekToEnd()\n        _ = try? fh.write(contentsOf: data)\n    } else {\n        try? data.write(to: url)\n    }\n}\n"
}

Data()\n    if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path),\n       let fh = try? {
  "path": "ios/README.md",
  "instructions": "README documenting the port, build steps, and publishing caveat",
  "content": "# Jantar Mantar Chalo — iOS / iPadOS / Mac Catalyst\n\nPort of the Android app (`com.jantarmantarchalo.app`). Like the Android version,\nthis is a native shell around the game's web client:\n\n- **Online**: loads the live game at `https://mantarjantar.com` (multiplayer\n  works, same server as the website and the Android app).\n- **Offline**: if the live site can't be reached, it automatically falls back to\n  the bundled copy of the web client (`WebAssets/`), where **Practice (offline)**\n  mode works with no network at all — served through a custom `jmclocal://`\n  `WKURLSchemeHandler` (the equivalent of Android's\n  `WebViewAssetLoader` + `appassets.androidplatform.net`).\n- The user agent gets `JantarMantarChaloApp` appended (same trick as the\n  Android app) so the page hides its \"Get the Android app\" banner.\n- Screen stays awake during play; status bar hidden; all orientations supported.\n\n## Project layout\n\n```\nios/\n  project.yml            # XcodeGen manifest (regenerate with `xcodegen generate`)\n  Sources/               # SwiftUI + WKWebView wrapper\n  WebAssets/             # bundled copy of the web game (from ../assets)\n```\n\n## Build & run\n\nRequires Xcode. The `.xcodeproj` is generated by [XcodeGen](https://github.com/yonaskolb/XcodeGen):\n\n```sh\ncd ios\nxcodegen generate          # after changing project.yml or adding files\nopen JantarMantarChalo.xcodeproj\n```\n\n- **iOS/iPadOS**: select the `JantarMantarChalo` scheme + an iOS Simulator or\n  device. For a device/App Store build, set your team & bundle id\n  (Signing & Capabilities); the default id is `com.jantarmantarchalo.app`.\n- **Mac Catalyst**: destination \"My Mac (Mac Catalyst)\" — supported via\n  `SUPPORTS_MACCATALYST`. Cmd+R reloads the game.\n\nDebug hooks:\n- Launch argument `-JMCOffline` forces the bundled copy (tests the offline path).\n- Debug builds log navigation/fallback to `tmp/jmc.log` in the app container\n  (`xcrun simctl get_app_container <device> com.jantarmantarchalo.app data`).\n\n## ⚠️ Publishing\n\nThe game (code, art, content, and its multiplayer server) belongs to its\ndeveloper (Suraj Kushwah — bugswriter.com). Before distributing an App Store\nbuild, get explicit permission; you'll also need the right to point the app at\n`mantarjantar.com` or host your own server.\n"
}
````

Done. The port lives in `ios/` and replicates exactly what the Android wrapper does — it's a native shell around the game's web client:

**What was built**
- `ios/Sources/JantarMantarChaloApp.swift` — SwiftUI entry, fullscreen, status bar hidden, Cmd+R reload on Mac Catalyst
- `ios/Sources/GameWebView.swift` — WKWebView that loads `https://mantarjantar.com` (live multiplayer), with automatic fallback to the bundled game when the site is unreachable; UA gets `JantarMantarChaloApp` appended so the page hides its "Get the Android app" bar; keeps screen awake; opens `target=_blank` links outside the app
- `ios/Sources/LocalAssetSchemeHandler.swift` — `WKURLSchemeHandler` serving `WebAssets/` under `jmclocal://` (the iOS equivalent of Android's `WebViewAssetLoader`)
- `ios/project.yml` — XcodeGen manifest: iOS 16+, iPhone/iPad, `SUPPORTS_MACCATALYST: true`; `.xcodeproj` generated with the `xcodegen` you already have

**Verified, not just built**
- ✅ iOS Simulator build + Mac Catalyst build (Xcode-beta 27.1) succeed
- ✅ Ran in the iPhone 17 Pro Max simulator: menu renders with the game's full UI (pixel-checked the paper card + saffron play button)
- ✅ Offline fallback verified end-to-end via debug log: live load → TLS error → automatic switch to bundled assets → `index.html`, `world.js`, `sim.js`, `snapcodec.js`, `client.js` all served → page finished
- ⚠️ One environment caveat: **this network blocks mantarjantar.com** (a content filter MITMs it — other HTTPS sites work fine), so the live multiplayer page couldn't load *here*. On an unfiltered network it loads directly — same URL the website and Android app use. Practice-offline mode needs no network and is fully verified.

**Reminder:** the game, its art/content, and its server belong to Suraj Kushwah (bugswriter.com) — get his permission before shipping to the App Store, and note online play depends on his server (or your own hosted copy).

- path: ios/Sources/GameWebView.swift
  content: |
      
          final class Coordinator: NSObject, WKNavigationDelegate {
              weak var webView: WKWebView?
              private var usingLocalFallback = false
      
              override init() {
                  super.init()
                  NotificationCenter.default.addObserver(
                      self, selector: #selector(reloadGame), name: .jmcReload, object: nil
                  )
              }
      
              @objc private func reloadGame() {
                  usingLocalFallback = false
                  loadInitial()
              }
      
              func loadInitial() {
                  if ProcessInfo.processInfo.arguments.contains("-JMCOffline") {
                      // Debug hook: force the bundled copy (used to test the offline path).
                      usingLocalFallback = true
                      jmcLog("[JMC] loading local fallback (forced)")
                      webView?.load(LocalAssetSchemeHandler.localIndexRequest())
                  } else {
                      jmcLog("[JMC] loading live site")
                      webView?.load(URLRequest(url: GameWebView.liveURL))
                  }
              }
      
              func webView(
                  _ webView: WKWebView, didFinish navigation: WKNavigation!
              ) {
                  jmcLog("[JMC] didFinish: \(webView.url?.absoluteString ?? "nil")")
              }
      
              // The live site can't be reached (no network, DNS failure, timeout):
              // switch to the bundled copy so Practice offline still works.
              func webView(
                  _ webView: WKWebView, didFailProvisionalNavigation navigation: WKNavigation!,
                  withError error: Error
              ) {
                  let ns = error as NSError
                  jmcLog("[JMC] didFailProvisionalNavigation domain=\(ns.domain) code=\(ns.code) url=\(webView.url?.absoluteString ?? "nil") failing=\(ns.userInfo[NSURLErrorFailingURLErrorKey] as? URL as Any) desc=\(ns.localizedDescription)")
                  guard ns.domain == NSURLErrorDomain,
                        ns.code != NSURLErrorCancelled,
                        !usingLocalFallback,
                        Bundle.main.url(forResource: "WebAssets", withExtension: nil) != nil
                  else { return }
                  let failedURL = (ns.userInfo[NSURLErrorFailingURLErrorKey] as? URL) ?? webView.url
                  guard failedURL?.host?.hasSuffix("mantarjantar.com") == true else {
                      jmcLog("[JMC] not falling back: failing host was \(failedURL?.host ?? "nil")")
                      return
                  }
                  usingLocalFallback = true
                  jmcLog("[JMC] falling back to bundled assets")
                  webView.load(LocalAssetSchemeHandler.localIndexRequest())
              }
      
              // Links with target="_blank" (e.g. "About the game") open outside the app.
              func webView(
                  _ webView: WKWebView, createWebViewWith configuration: WKWebViewConfiguration,
                  for navigationAction: WKNavigationAction,
                  windowFeatures: WKWindowFeatures
              ) -> WKWebView? {
                  if navigationAction.targetFrame == nil, let url = navigationAction.request.url {
                      UIApplication.shared.open(url)
                  }
                  return nil
              }
      
              func webView(
                  _ webView: WKWebView, decidePolicyFor navigationAction: WKNavigationAction,
                  decisionHandler: @escaping (WKNavigationActionPolicy) -> Void
              ) {
                  guard let url = navigationAction.request.url else {
                      decisionHandler(.cancel)
                      return
                  }
                  jmcLog("[JMC] decidePolicy: \(url.absoluteString)")
                  let scheme = url.scheme?.lowercased() ?? ""
                  if ["http", "https", LocalAssetSchemeHandler.scheme, "about", "blob", "data"].contains(scheme) {
                      decisionHandler(.allow)
                  } else {
                      UIApplication.shared.open(url)
                      decisionHandler(.cancel)
                  }
              }
          }
      }

  content: |
      import Foundation
      
      /// Tiny file logger for debugging in the simulator (visible via
      /// `simctl get_app_container <device> <bundle-id> data` + tmp/jmc.log).
      func jmcLog(_ message: String) {
          #if DEBUG
          let url = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent("jmc.log")
          let data = (message + "\n").data(using: .utf8) ?? Data()
          if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path),
             let fh = try? FileHandle(forWritingTo: url) {
              defer { try? fh.close() }
              _ = try? fh.seekToEnd()
              _ = try? fh.write(contentsOf: data)
          } else {
              try? data.write(to: url)
          }
          #endif
      }

```json
{
  "path": "ios/Tools/make_icon.py",
  "instructions": "Generate a 1024x1024 cockroach app icon (flat cartoon style matching the game's art), saved into the asset catalog",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Generate the app icon: a cartoon cockroach on the game's paper background,\nwith the tricolour stripe used on the game's menu card. Draws at 4x and\ndownsamples for smooth edges. Output: Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/icon1024.png\n\"\"\"\nimport math\nimport os\nfrom PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFilter\n\nS = 4                    # supersample factor\nW = 1024 * S             # canvas\n\nINK = (35, 24, 15)       # game outline colour rgba(35,24,15)\nPAPER = (246, 238, 221)  # #f6eedd\nPAPER_DARK = (228, 216, 190)\nSAFFRON = (255, 153, 51)\nGREEN = (19, 136, 8)\nWHITE = (255, 255, 255)\nBODY_DARK = (58, 34, 16)   # head/legs\nBODY_MID = (109, 63, 26)   # pronotum\nBODY = (141, 90, 43)       # wings\nBODY_LIGHT = (169, 113, 61)\n\nimg = Image.new(\"RGBA\", (W, W), PAPER + (255,))\nd = ImageDraw.Draw(img)\n\n# subtle radial vignette on the paper\nvig = Image.new(\"L\", (W, W), 0)\nvd = ImageDraw.Draw(vig)\nvd.ellipse([-W * 0.35, -W * 0.35, W * 1.35, W * 1.35], fill=70)\nvig = vig.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(W // 8))\nimg = Image.composite(Image.new(\"RGBA\", (W, W), PAPER_DARK + (255,)), img, vig)\nd = ImageDraw.Draw(img)\n\n# tricolour stripe across the top (matches the menu card)\nstripe_h = int(74 * S)\nthird = W / 3\nfor i, col in enumerate((SAFFRON, WHITE, GREEN)):\n    d.rectangle([i * third, 0, (i + 1) * third, stripe_h], fill=col + (255,))\nd.rectangle([0, stripe_h, W, stripe_h + 6 * S], fill=INK + (40,))\n\nCX = W // 2\nOL_W = int(7 * S)\n\n\ndef bez(p0, p1, p2, n=40):\n    \"\"\"quadratic bezier points\"\"\"\n    pts = []\n    for i in range(n + 1):\n        t = i / n\n        x = (1 - t) ** 2 * p0[0] + 2 * (1 - t) * t * p1[0] + t ** 2 * p2[0]\n        y = (1 - t) ** 2 * p0[1] + 2 * (1 - t) * t * p1[1] + t ** 2 * p2[1]\n        pts.append((x, y))\n    return pts\n\n\ndef taper(pts, w0, w1, col):\n    \"\"\"draw a polyline with tapering width + round joints\"\"\"\n    n = len(pts) - 1\n    for i in range(n):\n        w = w0 + (w1 - w0) * (i / max(1, n))\n        d.line([pts[i], pts[i + 1]], fill=col, width=max(1, int(w)))\n        r = w / 2\n        d.ellipse([pts[i][0] - r, pts[i][1] - r, pts[i][0] + r, pts[i][1] + r], fill=col)\n\n\n# ---- soft drop shadow\nsh = Image.new(\"RGBA\", (W, W), (0, 0, 0, 0))\nsd = ImageDraw.Draw(sh)\nsd.ellipse([CX - 280 * S, 760 * S, CX + 280 * S, 862 * S], fill=(20, 12, 5, 90))\nsh = sh.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(18 * S))\nimg.alpha_composite(sh)\nd = ImageDraw.Draw(img)\n\n# ---- legs (3 per side, behind body), angular like the game sprites\nlegs = [\n    ((455, 405), (330, 320), (238, 285)),\n    ((450, 490), (296, 480), (192, 532)),\n    ((455, 580), (318, 655), (228, 745)),\n]\nfor hx, ky, fy in legs:\n    for sgn in (-1, 1):\n        pts = [(CX + (p[0] - 512) * sgn * S, p[1] * S) for p in (hx, ky, fy)]\n        # outline pass then colour pass\n        taper(pts, 34 * S, 12 * S, INK + (255,))\n        taper(pts, 22 * S, 6 * S, BODY_DARK + (255,))\n\n# ---- cerci (two little tail spikes)\nfor sgn in (-1, 1):\n    pts = [(CX + sgn * 55 * S, 745 * S), (CX + sgn * 95 * S, 815 * S)]\n    taper(pts, 16 * S, 5 * S, INK + (255,))\n    taper(pts, 9 * S, 3 * S, BODY_MID + (255,))\n\n# ---- antennae\nfor sgn in (-1, 1):\n    pts = bez((CX + sgn * 28 * S, 300 * S), (CX + sgn * 200 * S, 170 * S), (CX + sgn * 330 * S, 215 * S))\n    taper(pts, 20 * S, 6 * S, INK + (255,))\n    taper(pts, 12 * S, 3 * S, BODY_MID + (255,))\n\n# ---- abdomen / wings (big oval)\nabd = [CX - 205 * S, 385 * S, CX + 205 * S, 775 * S]\nd.ellipse(abd, fill=BODY + (255,), outline=INK + (255,), width=OL_W)\n# gradient-ish shading: darker lower half\ngrad = Image.new(\"RGBA\", (W, W), (0, 0, 0, 0))\ngd = ImageDraw.Draw(grad)\ngd.ellipse(abd, fill=BODY_DARK + (0,))\ngrad.putalpha(Image.composite(grad.getchannel(\"A\"), Image.new(\"L\", (W, W), 0),\n                              Image.linear_gradient(\"L\").rotate(270, expand=True)\n                              .crop((0, 385 * S, W, 775 * S)).resize((W, W))))\nmask = Image.new(\"L\", (W, W), 0)\nImageDraw.Draw(mask).ellipse(abd, fill=255)\ngrad.putalpha(Image.composite(grad.getchannel(\"A\"), Image.new(\"L\", (W, W), 0), mask))\nimg.alpha_composite(grad)\nd = ImageDraw.Draw(img)\n\n# wing centre seam + fold lines\nd.line([(CX, 425 * S), (CX, 745 * S)], fill=INK + (150,), width=int(5 * S))\nfor sgn in (-1, 1):\n    fold = bez((CX + sgn * 70 * S, 430 * S), (CX + sgn * 190 * S, 560 * S), (CX + sgn * 95 * S, 735 * S))\n    taper(fold, 5 * S, 4 * S, INK + (120,))\n\n# glossy highlight on the wings\nhl = Image.new(\"RGBA\", (W, W), (0, 0, 0, 0))\nImageDraw.Draw(hl).ellipse([CX - 165 * S, 430 * S, CX - 35 * S, 620 * S], fill=(255, 255, 255, 55))\nhl = hl.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(10 * S))\nimg.alpha_composite(hl)\nd = ImageDraw.Draw(img)\n\n# ---- pronotum shield\nd.ellipse([CX - 150 * S, 320 * S, CX + 150 * S, 460 * S], fill=BODY_MID + (255,),\n          outline=INK + (255,), width=OL_W)\nd.ellipse([CX - 105 * S, 340 * S, CX + 105 * S, 405 * S], fill=BODY_LIGHT + (255,))\n\n# ---- head\nd.ellipse([CX - 82 * S, 245 * S, CX + 82 * S, 355 * S], fill=BODY_DARK + (255,),\n          outline=INK + (255,), width=OL_W)\n\n# ---- eyes (cartoon-friendly) + highlights\nfor sgn in (-1, 1):\n    ex = CX + sgn * 44 * S\n    ey = 292 * S\n    r = 22 * S\n    d.ellipse([ex - r, ey - r, ex + r, ey + r], fill=(18, 12, 8, 255))\n    hr = 7 * S\n    d.ellipse([ex - hr - 4 * S, ey - hr - 5 * S, ex + hr - 4 * S, ey + hr - 5 * S], fill=WHITE + (255,))\n\n# ---- tiny smile\nd.arc([CX - 26 * S, 315 * S, CX + 26 * S, 345 * S], 20, 160, fill=(200, 170, 140, 255), width=int(5 * S))\n\nout = img.convert(\"RGB\").resize((1024, 1024), Image.LANCZOS)\nhere = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\ndest = os.path.join(here, \"..\", \"Assets.xcassets\", \"AppIcon.appiconset\")\nos.makedirs(dest, exist_ok=True)\npath = os.path.join(dest, \"icon1024.png\")\nout.save(path, \"PNG\")\nprint(\"wrote\", os.path.normpath(path), out.size, out.mode)\n"
}
```

Icon compiled correctly for both phone and pad idioms. One last sanity check — a coarse ASCII "view" of the icon to confirm the roach shape reads properly, plus the generated bundle PNGs:

**🛠 Run Terminal Command**

```json
{
  "command": "export DEVELOPER_DIR=/Applications/Xcode-beta.app/Contents/Developer\nAPP=$(ls -d ~/Library/Developer/Xcode/DerivedData/JantarMantarChalo-*/Build/Products/Debug-iphonesimulator/JantarMantarChalo.app | head -1)\npython3 - <<'EOF'\nfrom PIL import Image\ndef classify(c):\n    r,g,b = c\n    if r>230 and g>225 and b>200: return 'P'      # paper\n    if r>230 and 120<g<190 and b<90: return 'S'   # saffron\n    if r<80 and 100<g<180 and b<60: return 'G'    # green\n    if r>240 and g>240 and b>240: return 'W'      # white\n    if r<90 and g<70 and b<60: return 'K'         # ink/dark brown\n    if 100<r<200 and 60<g<130 and b<90: return 'b' # mid brown\n    return '?'\nfor path,label in [('/tmp/icon1024.png','source'), (None,'bundle60x60@2x')]:\n    im = Image.open('ios/Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/icon1024.png').convert('RGB') if path else None\n    if im is None:\n        import glob\n        p = glob.glob('/Users/shashwath/Library/Developer/Xcode/DerivedData/JantarMantarChalo-*/Build/Products/Debug-iphonesimulator/JantarMantarChalo.app/AppIcon60x60@2x.png')[0]\n        im = Image.open(p).convert('RGB')\n    small = im.resize((28, 28), Image.LANCZOS)\n    print('==', label, im.size)\n    px = small.load()\n    for y in range(28):\n        print(''.join(classify(px[x,y]) for x in range(28)))\nEOF",
  "process_type": "SYNC",
  "timeout_seconds": 60
}
```

**What was added**
- `ios/Tools/make_icon.py` — reproducible generator (draws at 4× and downsamples); no image-gen API keys were available in this environment, so it's drawn procedurally in the game's flat style
- `ios/Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/icon1024.png` — 1024×1024, RGB **no alpha** (App Store requirement)
- `project.yml` — added the asset catalog + `ASSETCATALOG_COMPILER_APPICON_NAME: AppIcon`

**Verified**
- ✅ iOS Simulator and Mac Catalyst builds still succeed with the icon wired in
- ✅ `assetutil` on the built `Assets.car`: `AppIcon` present for both `phone` and `pad` idioms (1024×1024)

- ✅ Visual structure check (ASCII pixel map of both the source and the bundle-rendered icon): tricolour stripe, antennae, dark head with eyes, pronotum shield, oval winged body, six splayed legs, tail cerci — symmetric and readable at small size
